# Awesome X402 APIs [![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://awesome.re)

<!--lint disable double-link awesome-license awesome-list-item-->

[![Licence: CC0-1.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/licence-CC0--1.0-lightgrey.svg)](LICENSE) [![Settlement-verified](https://img.shields.io/badge/listings-settlement--verified-2ea44f.svg)](https://payapi.market/marketplace) [![Pay in USDC](https://img.shields.io/badge/pay-USDC%20on%20Base%2C%20Solana%20or%20Algorand-0052ff.svg)](https://payapi.market/agents)

> The best x402 APIs are pay-per-call APIs that an AI agent can pay in USDC over HTTP 402 and be sure of getting a real result. This list includes only x402 APIs that [PayAPI Market](https://payapi.market) has paid itself and received genuine output from, each with an on-chain receipt anyone can check.

Updated: 2026-11-21. Agents pay per call in USDC on Base, Solana or Algorand, with no API key or account. Each API links to its PayAPI Market listing, where you will find the endpoint, price and settlement receipt.

## Top picks

Six per category. Full method: [payapi.market/best-x402-apis-2027](https://payapi.market/best-x402-apis-2027).

- **Data:** [LaborPulse](https://payapi.market/api/laborpulse), [ProviderSignal Dental Market Intelligence](https://payapi.market/api/providersignal-dental-market-intelligence), [Parallax](https://payapi.market/api/parallax), [CSV Drift Evidence](https://payapi.market/api/csv-drift-evidence), [HALOWERK EV Charging](https://payapi.market/api/halowerk-ev-charging), [CanonicalHash, JSON Canonicalization API](https://payapi.market/api/canonicalhash-json-canonicalization-api)
- **Finance:** [SameDayDesk Machine Commerce APIs](https://payapi.market/api/samedaydesk-machine-commerce-apis), [Crypto Snapshot Pro - AI Trading Signals](https://payapi.market/api/crypto-snapshot-pro-ai-trading-signals), [MacroPulse](https://payapi.market/api/macropulse), [Cognilode Ticket Resale Underwriter](https://payapi.market/api/cognilode-ticket-resale-underwriter), [ATLAS SEC Filing Delta](https://payapi.market/api/atlas-sec-filing-delta), [Crypto Technicals & Web Utilities](https://payapi.market/api/crypto-technicals-web-utilities)
- **Intelligence:** [zFinia machine_economy_changes_since](https://payapi.market/api/zfinia-machine-economy-changes-since), [ot-intel-api](https://payapi.market/api/ot-intel-api), [TELESINT](https://payapi.market/api/telesint), [AgentPay](https://payapi.market/api/agentpay), [DNS Inspector record lookup](https://payapi.market/api/dns-inspector-record-lookup), [MCR Verified Demand Gap](https://payapi.market/api/mcr-verified-demand-gap)
- **Tools:** [Lemon Toolshed](https://payapi.market/api/lemon-toolshed), [kino402](https://payapi.market/api/kino402), [Bounty Engineer Agent Commerce API](https://payapi.market/api/bounty-engineer-agent-commerce-api), [x402 MCP Tools](https://payapi.market/api/x402-mcp-tools), [zFinia Accessibility Contrast Calculator](https://payapi.market/api/zfinia-accessibility-contrast-calculator), [Flat-rate LLM and EVM toolkit](https://payapi.market/api/flat-rate-llm-and-evm-toolkit)
- **Verification:** [MachineMint, Unsigned Base Transaction Preflight](https://payapi.market/api/machinemint-unsigned-base-transaction-preflight), [NexusEval Marketing Preflight](https://payapi.market/api/nexuseval-marketing-preflight), [10x402](https://payapi.market/api/10x402), [Contract Powers](https://payapi.market/api/contract-powers), [Novarithm Trust & Commerce Checks](https://payapi.market/api/novarithm-trust-commerce-checks), [SendCheck](https://payapi.market/api/sendcheck)

## Developer tools and SDKs

SDKs, facilitators and developer tools are listed in [xpaysh/awesome-x402](https://github.com/xpaysh/awesome-x402). Protocol documentation is at [x402.org](https://x402.org).

## How APIs get on this list

An API appears in the category tables only if, when the list was generated, it was:

1. live on PayAPI Market and settlement-verified: PayAPI paid one of its routes from its own wallet, received real output and recorded the on-chain USDC receipt (the "tx" link);
2. listed by an independent provider. Directory imports whose providers have not signed up ("Unclaimed (bazaar)") and first-party PayAPI Market and PayAPI House listings are not included.

Each provider shows at most five APIs in each table. Verification covers the paid route, not every route of an API, and is not an endorsement. The tables are regenerated from the public catalogue at `https://payapi.market/agent/list`.

Data report: [The x402 API market: a snapshot from PayAPI Market, October 2026 (Zenodo, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.23658608)](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23658608).

## Add your API

List it free at [payapi.market/providers](https://payapi.market/providers). There is no fee and no plan to choose: agents pay your endpoint directly and you keep 100%. Once a test payment settles and the API meets the rules above, it appears here at the next regeneration. For x402 APIs you do not want on PayAPI Market, open a pull request against `data/external.md` as described in [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).
